```java
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

```java
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;

    //@  assigns \nothing;

        if (pat.length() > txt.length())

        int i = 0;
```

```java
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

```

```java
    //@ ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 1 <= i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>

    //@          \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < A.length;

    //@                \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j]));
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= m;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 1 <= k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
        //@ loop_writes i;

                return null;
            }

        //@ decreases m - i;

            //@ maintaining (\forall int y; 0 <= y < j; ret[i][y] == A[m - 1 - i][n - 1 - y]);
            //@ loop_writes ret[i][*], j;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
```

```java
//@ requires true;
//@ ensures (A == null || A.length == 0 || A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0 || 
//@          (\exists int i; 1 <= i < A.length; A[i] == null || A[i].length != A[0].length)) ==> \result == null;
//@ ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
//@          (\forall int i; 1 <= i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>
//@         (\result != null &&
//@          \result.length == A.length &&
//@          \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
```
```java
//@          \fresh(\result) &&
//@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < A.length;
```
```java
//@             \result[i] != null && \result[i].length == A[i].length) &&
//@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < A.length;
//@             \forall int j; 0 <= j < A[0].length;
//@                \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j]));
//@ assignable \nothing;
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```java
/*@ public normal_behavior
  @ requires true;
  @ ensures (A == null || A.length == 0 || A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0 ||
  @          (\exists int i; 1 <= i && i < A.length; A[i] == null || A[i].length != A[0].length)) ==> \result == null;
  @ ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
  @          (\forall int i; 1 <= i && i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>
  @         (\result != null &&
  @          \result.length == A.length &&
  @          \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
  @          \fresh(\result) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             \result[i] != null && \result[i].length == A[i].length) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
  @                \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j]));
  @ assignable \nothing;
  @*/
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

/*@ public normal_behavior
  @ requires true;
  @ ensures (A == null || A.length == 0 || A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0 ||

```java
  @ ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
  @          (\forall int i; 1 <= i && i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>
  @         (\result != null &&
  @          \result.length == A.length &&
  @          \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
  @          \fresh(\result) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             \result[i] != null && \result[i].length == A[i].length) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
  @                \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j]));
  @ assignable \nothing;
  @*/
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```java
/*@ public normal_behavior
  @ requires true;
  @ ensures (A == null || A.length == 0 || A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0 ||
  @          (\exists int i; 1 <= i && i < A.length; A[i] == null || A[i].length != A[0].length)) ==> \result == null;
  @ ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
  @          (\forall int i; 1 <= i && i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>
  @         (\result != null &&
  @          \result.length == A.length &&
  @          \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
  @          \fresh(\result) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             \result[i] != null && \result[i].length == A[i].length) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
  @                \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j]));
  @ assignable \nothing;
  @*/
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

/*@ public normal_behavior
  @ requires true;
  @ ensures (A == null || A.length == 0 || A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0 ||

```java
  @ ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
  @          (\forall int i; 1 <= i && i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>
  @         (\result != null &&
  @          \result.length == A.length &&
  @          \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
  @          \fresh(\result) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             \result[i] != null && \result[i].length == A[i].length) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
  @                \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j]));
  @ assignable \nothing;
  @*/
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

/*@ public normal_behavior
  @ requires true;

```java
  @          (\forall int i; 1 <= i && i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>
  @         (\result != null &&
  @          \result.length == A.length &&
  @          \result[0].length == A[0].length &&

  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             \result[i] != null && \result[i].length == A[i].length) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
  @                \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j]));
  @ assignable \nothing;
  @*/
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```java
  @         (\result != null &&
  @          \result.length == A.length &&
  @          \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
  @          \fresh(\result) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             \result[i] != null && \result[i].length == A[i].length) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
  @                \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j]));
  @ assignable \nothing;
  @*/
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```java
  @             \result[i] != null && \result[i].length == A[i].length) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
  @                \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j])));
  @ assignable \nothing;
  @*/
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```java
  @          (\exists int i; 1 <= i && i < A.length; A[i] == null || A[i].length != A[0].length)) ==> \result == null;
  @ ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
  @          (\forall int i; 1 <= i && i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>
  @         (\result != null &&
  @          \result.length == A.length &&
  @          \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
  @          \fresh(\result) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             \result[i] != null && \result[i].length == A[i].length) &&
  @          (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @             (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
  @                \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j])));
  @ assignable \nothing;
  @*/
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

/*@ public normal_behavior

```java
  @   ensures (A == null || A.length == 0 || A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0 ||

  @   ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
  @            (\forall int i; 1 <= i && i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>
  @           (\result != null &&
  @            \result.length == A.length &&
  @            \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
  @            \fresh(\result) &&

  @               \result[i] != null && \result[i].length == A[i].length) &&
  @            (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @               (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
  @                  \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j])));
  @   assignable \nothing;
  @*/
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

  @   requires true;
  @   ensures (A == null || A.length == 0 || A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0 ||

```java
  @   ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
  @            (\forall int i; 1 <= i && i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>
  @           (\result != null &&
  @            \result.length == A.length &&
  @            \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
  @            \fresh(\result) &&
  @            (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @               \result[i] != null && \result[i].length == A[i].length) &&
  @            (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
  @               (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
  @                  \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j])));
  @   assignable \nothing;
  @*/
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

```java
/*@
  requires true;
  ensures (A == null || A.length == 0 || A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0 ||
           (\exists int i; 1 <= i && i < A.length; A[i] == null || A[i].length != A[0].length)) ==> \result == null;
  ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
           (\forall int i; 1 <= i && i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>
          (\result != null &&
           \result.length == A.length &&
           \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
           \fresh(\result) &&
           (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
              \result[i] != null && \result[i].length == A[i].length) &&
           (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length;
              (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
                 \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j])));
  assignable \nothing;
@*/
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```
